# libGLcore: the Odyssey driver routines in the other builds

The driver routines of [libglcore-odyssey-reads.md](libglcore-odyssey-reads.md) were read in the N32 build of the 6.5.30 `libGLcore.so` for IP30 (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32/libGLcore.so`). The same names exist in the other builds listed in [docs/inventory/binaries.tsv](inventory/binaries.tsv): the o32 and 64-bit IP30 builds, the IP35 builds and the vpro-patch builds. Every row here gives one routine in one build, at that build's own address and size from the function table ([docs/inventory/functions.tsv](inventory/functions.tsv)). No N32 offset is copied into a row.

A copy is either identical to the N32 routine or it is not.

- **Identical copies** are listed in the first table. For each, the 32-bit instruction words of the copy were taken from the build's own disassembly (`objdump -d`) over the copy's address range and compared word for word with the N32 routine's words over its range. The function table gives the same code hash for both. The N32 routine was read in full, so an identical copy carries the same reading and is **established**. The row does not claim anything about the copy beyond its words matching.
- **Copies that differ** are in the second table, one row per build at that build's own address. Each row gives the body that was read in full and how it differs from the N32 routine, using the copy's own context offsets. Routines not yet in that table still have unread copies in the o32 and 64-bit IP30 builds, the IP35 o32 and 64-bit builds and the vpro-patch builds.

## Identical copies

Words compared: the number of instruction words in the routine, in N32 and in the copy, and how many matched. The function-table hash is the 12-digit code hash the inventory assigns to the routine in that build; it is the same for the N32 routine and for every identical copy.

Notes on individual rows:

- `__glOdysseyValidateClipPlanes`: objdump prints `...` in place of two zero words at 0xd967f38 and 0xd967f3c, in the N32 build and in both copies. Those two words are not in the comparison. The 206 words shown match word for word.
- `MakeCurrentRead` and `DoDomain2WithDerivs` are **inferred** because their N32 entries are: the reads chapter says which parts were read in full. Their copies match the N32 words, so they take the N32 label.
- The copies under `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY32` and `usr/gfx/arch/IP35ODSY32` (the paths without the `builds/6.5.30-media` prefix) call through their own `.got`. The IP35 copy's `.got` is word for word the N32 build's. The IP30 copy's `.got` differs in 36 words, all for imported symbols (dynamic symbol indexes 4283 to 4319, for example `bcopy` at 4300 and `floor` at 4319). The name at every GOT index from 4281 up is the same in the N32 build and in this copy, so each call through a gp slot reaches the same routine.
- The words in these rows were compared on the file bytes at each address, as 32-bit big-endian words, which are the words objdump prints. None of the routines has a direct `j` or `jal`, so no call target is an absolute address that could mean something else in another build.
- The 64-bit copies of `ComputeFirstPartials` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64` at 0x21bb670, and `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64` and `usr/gfx/arch/IP35ODSY64` at 0x320b670 and 0x21bb670) have the same 39 words as the N32 routine. Its body has no gp-relative load and no call, so the words are the whole comparison.

## Copies that differ

Each row is one routine in one build. "Body read" is the byte range of the routine's code that was disassembled and read in full, which ends at the return. Where a copy's table size runs past its return into the next routine, the table size is given with the body read. A row whose code hash equals the hash of an earlier row in this table reads the same bytes, so it takes that row's reading.

Context offsets are the copy's own. Several builds keep the context record with different field positions (the 64-bit builds use 8-byte pointers in it), so a copy's offsets are not the N32 offsets.

| Routine | Binary | Address | Table size | Body read | Function-table hash | Label | How it differs from the N32 routine |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `__glOdysseyValidateScissor` | `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` (builds/6.5.30-media) | 0xd96c430 | 1392 | 360 bytes, 0xd96c430 to 0xd96c598 | dacb097cce56 | Established | Same steps as N32, and the same context offsets (shadow pair +0x7c20 and +0x7c24, saved pair +0x165b4 and +0x165b8, settle word +0x9830, bit-20 word +0x7b70). The routine starts with a PIC prologue that loads gp from t9 and saves gp and ra in a 56-byte frame. The ioctl argument block is copied from a zero-filled template in .data at 0xdaf8060 and each word's low 12 bits are then set to the field, so the four words sent equal the N32 block. The ioctl is called through the GOT slot at gp-30516, which holds 0xd9eb890, the address of `__glOdysseyIoctl` in this build. |
| `__glOdysseyValidateScissor` | `usr/gfx/arch/IP35ODSY/libGLcore.so` | 0xd96c430 | 1392 | same bytes as the IP30 o32 row | dacb097cce56 | Established | Same code hash as the IP30 o32 row above, so the same reading applies. |
| `__glOdysseyValidateScissor` | `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` (builds/6.5.30-media) | 0x21ada40 | 256 | 256 bytes, all | b1229c1bfe60 | Established | Same steps as N32 in 64-bit code (ld, sd and daddu). Its own offsets: shadow pair +0x9f70 and +0x9f74, saved pair +0x18a24 and +0x18a28, settle word +0xbb80, bit-20 word +0x9ec0. The shadow is updated from the saved pair before the settle test, the settle block sends 0x10443, 250, 0x10046 and 0x10046, and the four ioctl fields are built as in N32. The ioctl is called through the GOT slot at gp-28272, which holds 0x221da60, the address of `__glOdysseyIoctl` in this build. |
| `__glOdysseyValidateScissor` | `usr/gfx/arch/IP35ODSY64/libGLcore.so` | 0x21ada40 | 256 | same bytes as the IP30 64-bit row | b1229c1bfe60 | Established | Same code hash as the IP30 64-bit row above, so the same reading applies. |
| `__glOdysseyValidateScissor` | `builds/vpro-patch-4103/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` | 0xda48c88 | 1108 | 360 bytes, 0xda48c88 to 0xda48df0 | 0f6707a8cd52 | Established | Same steps as N32, with its own offsets: saved pair +0x16648 and +0x1664c, shadow pair +0x7b40 and +0x7b44, settle word +0x9750, bit-20 word +0x7a90. The FIFO stores go to absolute 0x4000 (`16384(zero)`), not to the 0x10000 window the IP30 builds use. The ioctl argument block comes from a zero-filled template in .data at 0xdbd1060, so the values equal the N32 values. The ioctl is called through the GOT slot at gp-30572, which holds 0xdabf6b0, the address of `__glOdysseyIoctl` in this build. |

The ioctl is called through the GOT slot at gp-28400, which holds 0xb3a1ef0, the address of `__glOdysseyIoctl` in this build. |

| `Bogus` | `builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` | 0xd976244 | 668 | 8 bytes, 0xd976244 to 0xd97624c | 2ea76de3acb7 | Established | Same two words as the N32 routine (`jr ra`, then `move v0,a0`), so it returns its argument unchanged as N32 does. The table size of 668 runs past the return into the following routines, and the hash covers those bytes too; that is why the hash differs from N32, not any difference in the body. |
| `__glOdysseyDlistOptimizer` | `builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` | 0xd974948 | 12 | 12 bytes, 0xd974948 to 0xd974954 | 691e766163b1 | Established | Not an empty return as in N32. It stores a0 at 0(sp) and a1 at 4(sp), the caller's argument save words, with the second store in the delay slot of `jr ra`, and nothing else. The N32 routine is `jr ra` and a nop with no stores. |
| `InitialFree` | `builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` | 0xd9687bc | 52 | 52 bytes, 0xd9687bc to 0xd9687f0 | 2f4829c14bb5 | Established | Not a thunk. It sets gp from t9 (lui 0x1a, addiu -9180, addu), saves ra and gp in a 32-byte frame, calls through the GOT slot at gp-32476 with `jalr t9`, restores ra and gp, pops the frame and returns. The slot holds `__glOdysseyFreeAttributeState` in this build (resolved from the build's GOT). a0 is not changed before the call. N32 jumps to the same callee with `jr t9`, so this copy returns through its own frame. |
| `InitialFree` | `builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` | 0x21aa0e0 | 12 | 12 bytes, 0x21aa0e0 to 0x21aa0ec | 356def7c4388 | Established | The same three-instruction thunk as N32, in 64-bit form: `ld t9,-32192(gp)`, `jr t9`, nop. The slot at gp-32192 (8-byte pointers in this build) holds `__glOdysseyFreeAttributeState`. N32 loads the slot at gp-32476. |
| `InitCSet` | `builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` | 0xd9d9b58 | 20 | 20 bytes, 0xd9d9b58 to 0xd9d9b6c | eff91331ed67 | Established | Same stores to the same words as N32 (zero at +0, +8 and +12, a1 at +24), in a different order: zero at +0, +12 and +8, then `jr ra`, with `sw a1,24(a0)` in the delay slot. |
| `InitCSet` | `builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` | 0x220c420 | 20 | 20 bytes, 0x220c420 to 0x220c434 | 931c1c1883c7 | Established | 64-bit layout. `sw a1,32(a0)` stores the shift at +32 (N32 has it at +24), `sw zero` writes words at +16 and +20 (N32: +8 and +12), and the head is cleared by an 8-byte `sd zero,0(a0)` in the delay slot of `jr ra`. |
| `InitCSetCursor` | `builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` | 0xd9d9f34 | 20 | 20 bytes, 0xd9d9f34 to 0xd9d9f48 | 2ab35aadc4ca | Established | Same stores as N32 (zero at +16 and +20, word 0 copied to word 4), in a different order: `lw t6,0(a0)`, `sw zero,16(a0)`, `sw zero,20(a0)`, `jr ra`, and `sw t6,4(a0)` in the delay slot. The copy uses t6 where N32 uses at. |
| `InitCSetCursor` | `builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so` | 0x220c7f0 | 20 | 20 bytes, 0x220c7f0 to 0x220c804 | f3a5e060bb32 | Established | 64-bit layout. Zero words at +24 and +28 (N32: +16 and +20), then `ld at,0(a0)` and an 8-byte `sd at,8(a0)` in the delay slot of `jr ra`, so the first pointer-sized word is copied from +0 to +8 (N32: +0 to +4). |

The copy uses the 64-bit forms (`daddiu`, `daddu`, `ld`). a3 is the 8-byte word at gp-32440 with +4776 added in the call's delay slot. The 8-byte load into t9 (gp-30536) is dead, as in N32. |
| `__glOdyim_GetIntegerv` | `builds/vpro-patch-4103/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` | 0xda68614 | 64 | 64 bytes, 0xda68614 to 0xda68654 | 8ba47f687c3a | Established | A different call and frame. gp is t9 plus 0x17682c (`lui gp,0x17`, `addiu gp,26668`, `addu gp,gp,t9`). The call is indirect: `lw t9` from gp-31692 (GOT local entry 265, whose value 0x0da67ea8 is `__glOdysseyDoGet` in this build), then `jalr t9`, with `li a2,3` before it. The frame is 32 bytes; ra and gp are saved with `sw` at 28 and 24, where N32 uses a 16-byte frame and `sd` at 0 and 8. a3 is the word at gp-32644 (GOT local entry 27, value 0x0dbc0000) with -25236 added in the jalr's delay slot, giving 0x0dbb9d6c, a link-time address in `.rodata`. ra and gp are reloaded with `lw`, and the frame is popped before `jr ra`. this copy has no branch-likely, unlike N32's `bgezall`. |

## Copies with the same code hash

Each row is one routine in one build whose function-table code hash equals the hash of the N32 routine named in the reads chapter ([libglcore-odyssey-reads.md](libglcore-odyssey-reads.md)). The hash is the first 12 hex digits of the hash of the function's bytes, taken over the table size, and every row here has the same table size as its N32 routine. The inventory computed each hash over that build's own bytes, so an equal hash means equal bytes and the copy carries the N32 reading. The label follows the N32 reading: **Established** when the N32 entry in the reads chapter is established throughout, **Inferred** when part of it is inferred (as in the first table). The matching was done by the inventory script; the copy was not disassembled row by row. One spot check was made by hand: `CheckMMXfrArgs`, 128 bytes at 0xd9c6ca0 in the N32 build and at 0x325b220 in the `usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64` build, 32 words, equal in objdump. Copies whose hash differs from the N32 routine are not listed here.

## Copies identical to a read copy

Each row is one routine in one build whose function-table code hash and size equal those of another copy of the same name that is already in this document, at its own address. The inventory computes each hash over that build's own bytes, so an equal hash and size mean equal bytes. The copy carries the reading of the copy it matches: the "Same bytes as" column names that copy's build and address, and the label is that copy's label. The rows were produced by a script that compared the function-table hashes, not by disassembling each copy in full. Two of them were checked by hand: `TargetIndexToTargetEnum` in `usr/gfx/arch/IP35ODSY/libGLcore.so` at 0xd95ac3c (80 bytes) and `__glOdyim_EvalCoord1dv` in `usr/gfx/arch/IP35ODSY64/libGLcore.so` at 0x21b9f60 (60 bytes) each have the same 32-bit instruction words, in objdump, as the matched copy. Routines whose copy differs from every read copy are not listed here.

## Copies read in full

Each entry is one routine in the o32 IP30 build (`usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so`, from `builds/6.5.30-media`), at that build's own address and size. The body was read in full, instruction by instruction, against the N32 routine named in [libglcore-odyssey-reads.md](libglcore-odyssey-reads.md), and each entry states how the two bodies differ. Offsets are the copy's own. **Established** means every difference the body has is stated; **Inferred** means part of the body was not compared.

### O32 IP30 routines read in full

- `sendBlenderOpTokens` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd950010, 128 bytes):
  **Established.** Same job as the N32 entry: the first, second and third arguments fill bits 15:13, 12:6 and 5:0 of the low 16 bits of the context word at +0x7c34, the dirty bit 0 of the word at +0x16960 is set, and process word 4120 is set to 2. The context is loaded from process word 4112. The copy has 32 instructions against N32's 26. It stores the +0x7c34 word three times (after the first field, after the second, and with the final value) where N32 stores once; the end state is the same. It also reads the word at context +11812 (0x2e24) and writes the same value back in the delay slot of the return, which N32 does not do. The field masks use the same constants as N32 (0xffff1fff, -8129 and -64).
- `DeallocCSetId` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd9da074, 136 bytes):
  **Established.** Same algorithm as the N32 entry: the shift count is the word at +24 of the set, the node is reached by walking the first-level chain count times (count is the index shifted right by that amount), the table slot at the index masked to the node's range is zeroed, and the node's used count (+4) and the set's element count (+12) are each decremented. Differences: the mask is computed before the walk (N32 computes it after); the walk takes the count modulo 4 first, one node per step, and then four nodes per pass; the exit is a branch-likely (`beql`) that runs the final table load in its delay slot. N32 walks one node per pass and has no branch-likely.
- `__glOdysseyInitIgloo` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd971010, 144 bytes):
  **Established.** Sets the word at context +0x16980 to 1, bit 10 (0x400) of +0x16964 and bit 0 of +0x16988, stores 2 in process word 0x1018, then calls the entry at context +0x2e40 with the context. Differences: the callee gets the caller's a1 unchanged. In N32 a1 holds 0x188920 at the call, but only because the routine uses a1 as scratch for the gp offset (`lui` 0x19, `addiu` -30432); no code there passes it as an argument on purpose, so the value is incidental. It writes the word at +0x2e24 back to itself before the call. After storing process word 0x1018 it reloads that word and makes the call only when it reads 2 (a `bnel`), where N32 calls unconditionally. The frame saves gp and ra with `sw`.
- `__glOdyim_EvalPoint1` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd978240, 128 bytes):
  **Established.** Same parameter as N32: the grid end at +0x790 when the index equals the division count at +0x798, otherwise the start at +0x78c plus index times ((end minus start) divided by the count). It then calls `__glOdysseyDoEvalCoord1` (0xd979994, reached through the slot at gp-31800) with the context. Differences: the difference and the division are computed on every path, where N32 computes them only when the index differs from the count; the parameter is returned as its bit pattern in a1 (`mfc1 a1,$f0`), where N32 passes it in f13; the frame saves ra and gp with `sw`; N32 also loads a slot at gp-31800 into t9 but calls the routine directly.
- `__glOdysseyDoEvalCoord1` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd979994, 128 bytes):
  **Established.** The same three calls as N32, in order: `GetCurrentState` (0xd979264) with dimension 1 and a record, the routine the table names `DoEvalCoord1` (0xd979624), and `RestoreCurrentState` (0xd979548) with the record. The three targets come from the slot at gp-32736 (value 0xd980000) minus 28060, 27100 and 27320. Differences: the copy keeps the context (a0) and the parameter (a1) in stack words 104 and 108 and reloads them for the second call, where N32 keeps the parameter in f30 across the first call and does not reload a1; the record is at sp+36 (N32 uses sp+0); the frame is 104 bytes. The parameter reaches the second call as the bit pattern in a1, which matches what `__glOdyim_EvalPoint1` passes in this build.
- `__glOdysseyInitFragmentLighting` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd98aa58, 136 bytes):
  **Inferred.** Writes the same five float words into the fragment light record as N32, at context +0x164b8, +0x164c8, +0x164d8, +0x164e4 and +0x164e8, and the same colour-material words at +0x165a8 (1032, 0x408) and +0x165ac (5634, 0x1602), and sets the word at +0x16970 to 1. Differences: the five floats (1.0, 1.0, -1.0, 180.0, 1.0, in the order of the five stores) are built from three immediates (0x3f800000, 0xbf800000, 0x43340000) inside a loop that runs once, where N32 loads three words from a constant table through a pc-relative address. The values in that N32 table were not compared with these immediates, so the **Inferred** label stands.
- `__glOdysseyAccumClose` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd9501c8, 128 bytes):
  **Established.** Same effect as N32: result 1; the image object numbers at +0x11ec0 and +0x11efc go back to -1, with their entry words at +0x11ec4 and +0x11f00 cleared, when they were set; the words at +0x12064 and +0x120ac go to -1 and the words at +0x12068 and +0x120b0 are cleared under the same tests; the byte at +0x120f5 is cleared on every path, as in N32. Differences: this build uses branch-likely (`beql`, `bnel`) for the skips and fills the delay slots with the next store or load; N32 uses plain `beq` with `nop` in the delay slot. Within the first block it writes -1 before zero, where N32 writes zero first. On one path it writes -1 to +0x12064 a second time, which is harmless.
- `__glOdyim_GetListParameterivSGIX` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd9748e0, 104 bytes):
  **Established.** Converts the integer at the caller's pointer (the third argument, a2) to float, calls `__glOdyim_GetListParameterfvSGIX` (0xd9747f8, through the slot at gp-32052) with a pointer to a float temporary, and stores the truncated result back as an integer through the pointer. Differences: the temporary is at sp+36 (N32 uses sp+0 and stores the float in the call's delay slot); the copy saves the pointer at sp+48 and reloads it after the call, where N32 keeps it in s8; the integer is moved with `mfc1` and stored with `sw`, where N32 stores the float register with `swc1`; this build calls through the slot with `jalr`, and N32 loads a slot at gp-32052 into t9 and calls the routine directly without using it.
- `BindHyperpipe` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd96f3fc, 120 bytes):
  **Established.** Same effect as N32: a pipe number other than -1 calls `__glOdysseyBindHyperpipe` (0xda09438, through the slot at gp-29220) with the context and the number, and then stores the number at context +0x21ef0 (0x20000 + 7920); -1 calls `__glOdysseyUnbindHyperpipe` (0xda094ac, slot at gp-29216) with the stored number and stores nothing. Differences: the copy saves the context and the number in stack words 32 and 36 across the bind call and reloads them for the store, where N32 keeps them in s7 and s0; the test for -1 is a `bne` that falls into the bind path, where N32 branches to the unbind path with `beq`; the unbind path loads the stored number into a1 in the delay slot of the call and returns with a `b` that restores gp.
- `MakeCurrent` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd96f388, 116 bytes):
  **Established.** Same job as the N32 entry: the context (a0) is saved at 40(sp), the host entry at context +60 is called with it and then the entry at +64, the first result is kept at 36(sp), and the routine calls `MakeCurrentRead` (0xd96e5b0, which the function table names for this build) with (context, first result, second result), returning that routine's v0. The last callee is reached through the slot at gp-32740 plus -6736, not a direct call. Differences: the N32 routine keeps the context in s0 and the first result in s8, and it ends with a branch-likely `bgezl` to 0xd96af00 (the delay slot pops the frame), so the N32 last call is a tail call; this copy makes an ordinary `jalr` and returns through its own epilogue (`lw ra,28(sp)`, `addiu sp,sp,40`). The frame is 40 bytes with ra at 28(sp) and gp at 24(sp), against N32's 32 bytes with ra at 0(sp).
- `__glOdyGetFromShadow` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd98bde8, 124 bytes):
  **Established.** Same job as the N32 entry: the shadow pointer is read from context +93476 (0x16d24, `lui t7,1` and `lw t7,27940(t7)`) before the hardware read and saved at 36(sp). It calls `__glOdyGetFromHardware` (0xd98b3f8, GOT entry 273 through gp-31660) with the arguments unchanged and returns its result when that result is negative. Otherwise it calls `__glOdyDecodeShadowFormat` (0xd98b4e4, GOT entry 274 through gp-31656) with a0 = the shadow pointer plus 20 times n, where n is the caller's a2 reloaded from 48(sp), a1 = the caller's a3 from 52(sp), and a2 = the caller's fifth argument from 56(sp). It returns the decode routine's v0. Differences: the N32 routine also passes a3 = 20n and a4 = n to the decode routine, and this copy sets only a0, a1 and a2. The frame is 40 bytes with ra at 28(sp), gp at 24(sp), and the caller's a2 and a3 spilled to 48(sp) and 52(sp), where N32 uses a 48-byte frame with a2, a3 and a4 stored at 0, 8 and 16(sp).
- `__glOdyim_GetFogFuncSGIS` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd98d468, 124 bytes):
  **Established.** Same job as the N32 entry: when the process word at 4120 is not 1, it copies 8 times the word at context +91184 (0x16430) bytes from context +91188 (0x16434) to the caller's pointer (a0, unchanged) with `memcpy` (GOT entry 3936 by the formula PLTGOT + 4*(local_gotno 3928 + dynsym 4285 - gotsym 4277)) and returns memcpy's v0. When that word is 1 it calls `__glSetError` (0xda4b3d8, GOT entry 1490) with 1282 and returns that result. The two words are read from process page 4096 with `lw t6,24(v1)` and `lw v0,16(v1)`, that is, from process addresses 4120 and 4112. Differences: the N32 routine tests the flag with `beq` and places its SetError call out of line at the end, while this copy tests with `bne` and makes the SetError call inline; the frame is 32 bytes with ra at 28(sp) and gp at 24(sp), against N32's 16 bytes with ra at 8(sp) and gp at 0(sp); the N32 routine reads the two words from absolute addresses with `lw ...,4112(zero)` and `lw ...,4120(zero)`.
- `QueryHyperpipeBestAttrib` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd96f474, 128 bytes):
  **Established.** Same result as the N32 entry: the pipe number stored at context +138992 (0x21ef0) is negative, or the register a2 is not 1, and the routine returns 92; otherwise it calls `__glOdysseyQueryHyperpipeBestRect` (0xda08fd0, GOT entry 875 through gp-29252) with a0 = the context, a1 unchanged, a2 = the caller's a3, a3 = the caller's fifth argument and the fifth argument of the callee = the caller's sixth, stored at 16(sp), and returns that routine's v0. Differences: the N32 routine tests the stored number with `bltz` in v0, and the copy loads it into t6 and tests with `bgez`; the callee's arguments are the same values, but the copy passes the caller's fifth and sixth arguments from the stack (56(sp) and 60(sp)) where N32 passes them in a4 and a5; the `lw a3,56(sp)` in the delay slot of the `bne` on a2 runs on both paths, so a3 is overwritten even when the call is not made; the frame is 40 bytes with ra at 36(sp) and gp at 32(sp), against N32's 16 bytes; the result on the call path is moved through v1 before the return.

### 64-bit IP30 routines read in full

Each entry is one routine in the 64-bit IP30 build (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`), at that build's own address and size, read instruction by instruction against the N32 routine named in [libglcore-odyssey-reads.md](libglcore-odyssey-reads.md). Context and process-page addresses are the 64-bit build's own: a pointer is 8 bytes here, so the process word the N32 build reads as a 4-byte word at 4112 is read at 4128. The inventory credits each entry by its binary path and address.

- `AddKnownGroup` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`, 0x220c2b0, 68 bytes):
  **Established.** Same 17 instructions in the same order as N32, with the 64-bit forms (`ld`, `sd`, `daddiu`) for the slot load, the frame and the saves. The call is to `AllocSpecificCSetId` at 0x220c680 (360 bytes in this build). The record's first word is loaded as a 32-bit word into a1 in the call's delay slot and loaded again as s8 after the call; v0 is 0 when the returned id equals that word and 1 otherwise, as in N32. The slot load into t9 and the `daddiu` on t9 are dead, as in N32.
- `DestroyKnownGroup` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`, 0x220c3e0, 60 bytes):
  **Established.** Calls `RemoveKnownGroup` at 0x220c300 (212 bytes in this build) with a1 set to the element (the a0 of entry) and a0 loaded from process address 4128 (8 bytes) in the call's delay slot. It returns the callee's v0 unchanged, as N32 does. The frame is 16 bytes with ra at 0(sp) and gp at 8(sp). The slot load into t9 is dead.
- `__glOdyim_EvalCoord1dv` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`, 0x21b9f60, 60 bytes):
  **Established.** Same steps as N32: the double at 0(a0) is loaded into f13 before the frame is set up, the context from process address 4128 goes in a0, and the call to `__glOdysseyDoEvalCoord1` (0x21bb5f0, 128 bytes) has the `cvt.s.d` narrowing in its delay slot. The slot load into t9 is dead. Differences: the 8-byte forms and the 4128 context address (N32 reads 4112 as a word).
- `__glOdyim_EvalCoord1fv` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`, 0x21b9fe0, 60 bytes):
  **Established.** Same steps as N32: a0 is copied to at, the context from process address 4128 goes in a0, and the call to `__glOdysseyDoEvalCoord1` (0x21bb5f0) has the `lwc1` of the float at 0(at) into f13 in its delay slot. The slot load into t9 is dead. Differences: the 8-byte forms and the 4128 address, as in the double routine.
- `__glOdyim_EvalCoord2d` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`, 0x21ba020, 64 bytes):
  **Established.** Same steps as N32: `mov.d f14,f13` and `cvt.s.d f13,f12` on the arguments, the context from process address 4128 in a0, and the call to `__glOdysseyDoEvalCoord2` (0x21c05c0, 140 bytes) with `cvt.s.d f14,f14` in its delay slot. Differences: the 8-byte forms and the 4128 address. The `mov.d` and `cvt.s.d` pairs are the same instructions as in N32.
- `__glOdyim_EvalCoord2f` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`, 0x21ba0b0, 60 bytes):
  **Established.** Same steps as N32: `mov.s f14,f13` and `mov.s f13,f12` on the arguments, the context from process address 4128 loaded in the call's delay slot, and the call to `__glOdysseyDoEvalCoord2` (0x21c05c0). Differences: the 8-byte forms and the 4128 address. The slot load into t9 is dead.
- `__glOdyim_EvalCoord2fv` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`, 0x21ba0f0, 64 bytes):
  **Established.** Same steps as N32: a0 is copied to at, the context from process address 4128 goes in a0, word 4(at) is loaded into f14 before the call, and word 0(at) into f13 in the call's delay slot. The call is to `__glOdysseyDoEvalCoord2` (0x21c05c0). Differences: the 4128 address (N32 reads 4112 as a word), the 8-byte forms and the dead slot load into t9.
- `__glOdyim_ListParameterfSGIX` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`, 0x21b6f50, 56 bytes):
  **Established.** Same steps as N32: the float argument is stored at 32(sp) with `swc1 f14` in the call's delay slot, a2 is set to sp+32, and the call is to `__glOdyim_ListParameterfvSGIX` (0x21b6e60, 236 bytes). Differences: the 64-bit forms (`daddiu`, `daddu`, `ld`), with the same 48-byte frame, and the slot load into t9 is dead.
- `__glOdyim_ListParameteriSGIX` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`, 0x21b6fe0, 64 bytes):
  **Established.** Same steps as N32: `mtc1` moves a2 to f0, `cvt.s.w` converts it, a2 is set to sp (`daddiu a2,sp,0`), the float is stored at 0(sp) in the call's delay slot, and the call is to `__glOdyim_ListParameterfvSGIX` (0x21b6e60). The 32-byte frame saves gp at 16(sp) and ra at 8(sp), as in N32. Differences: the 64-bit forms and the dead slot load into t9.
- `__glOdyim_ListParameterivSGIX` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`, 0x21b6f90, 68 bytes):
  **Established.** Same steps as N32: the integer at 0(a2) is loaded into v0, moved to f0 with `mtc1`, converted with `cvt.s.w`, and the call to `__glOdyim_ListParameterfvSGIX` (0x21b6e60) gets a2 = sp with `swc1 f0,0(sp)` in its delay slot. Differences: the 64-bit forms and the dead slot load into t9.
- `__glOdyle_End` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`, 0x21b8350, 64 bytes):
  **Established.** Same steps as N32: the record in a0 is kept in s8 across the call, the routine is called through t9 loaded from process address 4328 (`ld t9,4328(zero)`, an 8-byte pointer) with `move s8,a0` in the delay slot, and v0 is set to s8 on return. The N32 routine loads its pointer from process address 4220 (0x107c). Differences: the 4328 address (0x10e8), and the 8-byte forms.
- `__glOdyle_Begin` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`, 0x21b8300, 68 bytes):

For each level k from a2 down to 0 the routine reads the list head at a1 + 8k + 16, walks the list through the doubleword next pointer at node +0, and tests the word at node +20 with `slt` against a2. The first node whose count is not below a2 is returned in v0 (`jr ra` at 0x220c2a0). A negative a2 (`bltz` at 0x220c250) and a scan that finds no node both reach the tail call: the t9 load at 0x220c290 is followed by `bgezl zero` to `LookupCoresidRange` (0x220c010, 564 bytes), with a3 = a2 in its delay slot. Differences: the slot stride is 8 bytes (`dsll` by 3), the head is at +16 (N32 +12), and the node count is at +20 (N32 +12). The loop-back at 0x220c28c is a plain `bne`, and its delay slot (the t9 load) runs on every pass. N32 uses `bnel` (branch-likely), and its delay slot, the next head load, runs only when the branch is taken. This copy loads the head at the top of the loop on every pass.
- `sendBlenderOpTokens` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`, 0x2194000, 112 bytes):
  **Established.** Same job as N32. The first, second and third arguments fill bits 15:13, 12:6 and 5:0 of the low 16 bits of the word at context +40836 (0x9f84, `lui a3,1` gives context + 0x10000, then `lw a6,-24700(a3)`). The masks are the same as N32's (0xe000 cleared, 0x1fc0 cleared with -8129, -64 for the low six bits). The dirty bit 0 of the word at context +101972 (0x18e54, the `lui a4,2` and `daddu v1,v1,a4` base plus -29100) is set, and process word 4144 is set to 2 in the delay slot of `jr ra`. The context is a doubleword at process address 4128. Differences: the 8-byte forms and the process and context addresses above (N32 reads the context at 4112, the global at 4120, the blend word at +0x7c34 and the dirty word at +0x16960). The routine has 28 instructions against N32's 26; the extra ones are the doubleword context load and the second `lui`/`daddu` pair that forms the dirty-word base.
- `initBuffersForAccum` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`, 0x21946d0, 112 bytes):
  **Established.** Same 28 instructions in the same order as N32, with the same calls. It calls `initVisualNumComp` (0x2194510) by a direct `bgezall` whose delay slot loads the context as a doubleword from process address 4128. It then calls `__glOdysseyImageDescribeWindow` (0x21cd480) twice through the GOT slot at gp-30344 (local entry 301, `ld t9`): first with a0 = context + 82384 (0x141d0) and a1 = 1, then with a0 = context + 82448 (0x14210) and a1 = 0. Finally it stores the byte 1 at context + 0x144b9 (`lui a2,1`, `ori a2,a2,0x44b9`, `daddu`), with `sb a1,0(a2)` in the delay slot of `jr ra`. Differences: the doubleword forms, the context address at process address 4128 (N32 4112), the descriptor offsets 0x141d0, 0x14210 and the byte at 0x144b9 (N32 0x11e38, 0x11e74 and 0x120f5), and the callee's own address 0x21cd480 (N32 0xd989710).
- `sendBlenderColorTokensAccum24` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`, 0x2194070, 124 bytes):
  **Established.** Same job as N32: the low 12 bits of the argument are placed in bits 11:0 and in bits 23:12 of two context words, and the dirty bit and the global are set as in `sendBlenderOpTokens`. The context is loaded as a doubleword from process address 4128 and the two words are at context +40840 (0x9f88) and +40844 (0x9f8c). The words are built with the same masks as N32 (0x00fff000 for the shifted value, 0xff000fff to keep the top byte and bits 11:0, and -4096 to clear the low 12 bits). The store order is the same as N32: this copy stores +0x9f8c (the N32 +0x7c3c word) first and +0x9f88 (the N32 +0x7c38 word) second, as N32 stores +0x7c3c and then +0x7c38. The dirty word is at context +101972 and the global at process address 4144 (N32 4120). The routine has 31 instructions against N32's 29.
- `InitialDestroyContext` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`, 0x21aa0f0, 120 bytes):
  **Established.** Same 30 instructions and the same branch structure as N32. It reads the sharing ring pointer at context +102808 (0x19198) and the kernel record at context +102864 (0x191d0). If either is nonzero it calls `__glOdysseyUnSharePM` (0x220ac30) through the GOT slot at gp-29232 with a0 = context. Then it calls `InitialFree` (0x21aa0e0) by a direct `bgezall` with a0 = context, and it calls `__glDestroyContext` (0x2279d00) through the GOT slot at gp-20808 with a0 = context, returning its result. Differences: the doubleword forms, the two checked offsets (N32 +0x16c70 and +0x16c94), the gp constant built from `lui` 0x1a and `addiu` -12856, and the callee addresses and slots above (N32: UnSharePM 0xd9c66b0 through gp-30996, InitialFree 0xd965d20, __glDestroyContext 0xda34a60 through gp-26804).
- `__glOdysseyDoEvalCoord1` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`, 0x21bb5f0, 128 bytes):
  **Established.** Same 32 instructions in the same order as N32, with the same three direct calls. The record is at sp+0 in both (`daddiu a2,sp,0`). The first call is `GetCurrentState` (0x21baef0) with dimension 1 (`li a1,1`) and the record, and its delay slot saves the float argument in f31 (`mov.s f31,f13`). The second call is `DoEvalCoord1` (0x21bb230) with the context in a0 and the saved float restored into f13 in its delay slot. The third is `RestoreCurrentState` (0x21bb180) with the record in a1 (`daddiu a1,sp,0` in its delay slot), after `ldc1 f31` restores the saved value. Differences: the saved float is f31 with `sdc1 f31,72(sp)` (N32 saves f30); the doubleword frame and gp forms; the gp constant built from `lui` 0x19 and `addiu` -18232; and the t9 loads before each call, from gp-32720 here (local GOT entry 4, the slot N32 also loads, at its own gp offset), each followed by an add of the callee's offset in both builds; the callees are entered by `bgezall` at labels inside the same function and do not read t9, so the loads are dead in both.
- `__glOdyssey_ResolveContext` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY64/libGLcore.so`, 0x219edc0, 128 bytes):
  **Established.** Same 32 instructions in the same order as N32. The context is the doubleword at process address 4128 (`ld s1,4128(zero)`), and the two scratch-record pointers are at context +101688 (0x18d38, record A) and context +101696 (0x18d40, record B), reached as s0 = context + 0x20000 minus 29384 and 29376. It calls `PopAttributes` (0x219d0e0) directly with a0 = A and a1 = B. It then releases A and B through the context's release entry, the doubleword at context +24 (`ld t9,24(s1)`), called with a0 = context and a1 = A, then a1 = B. It zeroes B first (`sd zero` at 0x18d40) and then A (`sd zero` at 0x18d38). Differences: the doubleword forms; the context at process address 4128 (N32 4112); the two record offsets (N32 +0x168c8 and +0x168cc); the release entry at +24 (N32 +12); and the gp constant built from `lui` 0x1b and `addiu` -32520.

### O32 IP30 routines read from the body alone

The entries below cover routines that have no N32 counterpart entry in [libglcore-odyssey-reads.md](libglcore-odyssey-reads.md). Each was read from its own body in the o32 IP30 build (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so`) with the routine's fact sheet beside it, and every offset is this build's own. The async-marker routines share one state block at context +0x19ea8 (106152): two rings of 1025 sixteen-byte records, the first ring's head word at state +16400 and tail word at +16404, the second ring starting at state +16408, then four per-kind counters at context +0x21ed8, +0x21edc, +0x21ee0 and +0x21ee4 and a pending-marker word at +0x21ee8.

- `__glOdysseyInitAsyncState` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd958190, 8 bytes):
  **Established.** Does nothing that outlasts the call: the first instruction is the return, and its delay slot stores a0 into the caller's outgoing argument word at 0(sp). No context word, process word or return register is written, so the async state block is not touched here.
- `AsyncPollDequeue` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd958198, 148 bytes):
  **Established.** Takes the state block in a0 and a 64-bit key in a2 (high word) and a3 (low word), with two output pointers in the caller's stack words 16(sp) and 20(sp). It reads the head word at +16400 (+0x4010) and the tail word at +16404 and returns 0 when they are equal. Otherwise it forms the record at head*16 and compares the key with the record's words at +8 (high) and +12 (low) as unsigned values: if the key's high word is below the record's, or the high words are equal and the key's low word is below the record's, it returns 0 with nothing changed. When the key is at or above the record, it stores the record's word at +0 through the pointer at 16(sp), its word at +4 through the pointer at 20(sp), sets the head word to (head+1) modulo 1025 (the constant 1025 is built by `addiu` at 0xd9581e8; the remainder is taken with `div` and `mfhi`) and returns 1. No calls, no branch-likely.
- `__glOdysseyAsyncCheck` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd95822c, 356 bytes):
  **Established.** The second argument is masked with the word at context +1816 and the result must equal 1, 2, 4 or 8 exactly. For each of those it reads one counter and one limit: kind 1 uses the counter at context +0x21ed8 and the limit word at +3652, kind 2 the counter at +0x21edc and the limit at +3644, kind 4 the counter at +0x21ee0 and the limit at +3648, kind 8 the counter at +0x21ee4 and the limit at +3656. The result v1 is 1 when counter is below limit (unsigned compare), and only then is the counter stored back as counter+1; any other masked value leaves v1 at 1 and touches nothing. When v1 is zero the routine calls `__glSetError` with 1282 (through the local GOT slot at gp-26792, entry 1490) and returns the zero it saved at 39(sp); otherwise it returns v1. The final test at 0xd958358 is the only branch-likely instruction.
- `__glOdysseyAsyncDropMarker` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd958390, 484 bytes):
  **Established.** The second argument is masked with the word at context +1816 and must equal 1, 2, 4 or 8, or the routine returns at once. For kinds 1, 2 and 4 it decrements the counter at +0x21ed8, +0x21edc and +0x21ee0 respectively (kind 8 has none) and copies the pending-marker word at +0x21ee8 to sp+4, with zero at sp+0; every matching kind then stores zero to the word at +0x21ee8. If the copied word is nonzero it appends a record at the first ring's tail (the tail word at state +16404 advances by 1 modulo 1025), otherwise it moves the head back one place (the head word at +16400 becomes head+1024 modulo 1025) and writes the record there. Either way the record gets sp+0 (zero) at +8, the copied word at +12, the context words at +6120 and +6124 at +0 and +4, and the word that context +6124 points to is incremented. The second ring's code at 0xd9584f0 to 0xd958514 cannot run: its test depends on the word at sp+0, which every path has just set to zero, and the `bne` at 0xd9584ac on the same word is never taken (0xd9584e0 is unreachable). No calls.
- `__glOdyim_PollAsyncSGIX` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd958574, 340 bytes):
  **Established.** The first argument is a pointer for the marker id. The context is the word at process address 4112 (s0) and the word at 4120 is tested first: when it equals 1 the routine calls `__glSetError` with 1282 (local GOT entry 1490) and returns 0. Otherwise it reads the pointer at context +93484 (+0x16d2c), takes the key pair at its +8 and +12, and calls `AsyncPollDequeue` (0xd958198) on the state block at context +0x19ea8 with that pair, the caller's pointer as the first output and a local word at sp+60 as the second. If that returns 0 it makes the same call on the second ring at state +16408 with the pair at the pointer's +24 and +28. When neither ring yielded a record it returns 0 (the `beql` at 0xd958654). Otherwise it calls `__glNamesUnlockData` (GOT entry 1765) with the context and the word in sp+60, reads the word that sp+60 points to, and when that is below 2 calls `__glNamesDeleteRange` (entry 1768) with the context, the table at context +6128, the marker id read through the caller's pointer, and a count of 1. It returns 1.
- `__glOdyim_FinishAsyncSGIX` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd9586c8, 152 bytes):
  **Established.** Reads the context from process word 4112 (s0) and, when process word 4120 equals 1, calls `__glSetError` with 1282 (local GOT entry 1490) and returns 0. Otherwise it loops: `sginap` with 0 (global GOT slot 3935 at gp-17012), then a call through the function pointer at offset 1156 of the table whose address is the word at context +6132 (+0x17f4), with the routine's own argument unchanged; it repeats until that call returns nonzero and then returns that value. No branch-likely.
- `__glOdysseyIoctl` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd9eb890, 56 bytes):
  **Established.** Calls the C library's `ioctl` (global GOT slot 3965, gp-16892) with the word at context +31560 (+0x7b48) (the context is a0) as the first argument, and returns its result. The second and later arguments (a1 onward) pass through untouched, because nothing writes them before the call. It is the route every private board request in this build takes.
- `__glOdysseyGetHardwareShadowTest` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd9eb8c8, 60 bytes):
  **Established.** Loads the context from process word 4112 and calls `__glOdysseyGetHardwareShadow` (0xd9eb904, local GOT entry 560) with it and a zero pointer; the result of that call is returned. It takes no arguments of its own.
- `__glOdysseyGetHardwareShadow` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd9eb904, 96 bytes):
  **Established.** Issues private ioctl 0x6f64000c through `__glOdysseyIoctl` (0xd9eb890, local GOT entry 559) with the context (a0) and the second argument (a1) moved into a2 as the request's data pointer. When the call returns a negative value (the `bgez` at 0xd9eb938 is not taken) it calls the function at offset 20 of the context with the context and the string at 0xdaefcc0, "ODSY_CTXMGR_SAVE_HW_SHADOW: failed", and returns whatever that call returns. A non-negative result is returned as is.
- `__glOdysseyFlush` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd9ebcf8, 108 bytes):
  **Established.** Stores 15 words to the FIFO port at the absolute address 0x10000 (`lui v0,1`) and one context word, in this order: 0x10443, 250, 0x10046, 0x10046, 0x10019, 0x10443, 150, four times 0x10046, then the word at context +38960 (+0x9830) (0x9830, a0 plus 32767 plus 6193) is set to zero, then 0x10443, 250, 0x10046, 0x10046. That is the settle sequence the X module and the other GL routines send, followed by a repeat of its opening group. It makes no calls and returns nothing.
- `__glOdysseyIsTextureResident` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd9eb400, 128 bytes):
  **Established.** Takes the context in a0 and a texture object in a1. It picks the smaller, as signed values, of the object's words at +96 (+0x60) and +156. If the object's word at +1276 is -1 it returns 0. Otherwise it calls `__glOdysseyPMObjGroupResident` (0xd9d8d14, local GOT entry 445) with the context, the word at +1276, the object's word at +92 and the smaller value, and returns the low byte of the result.
- `__glOdysseyTextureSetDetailLevel` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd9e8788, 140 bytes):
  **Established.** Takes the texture state in a0 and a level in a1. A non-negative level is rejected: the routine calls `__glSetError` with 1281 (local GOT entry 1490) and changes nothing. A negative level is stored as it is in the word at +64 (+0x40); its negation, limited to 15 (the `slti` against 16 is followed by a branch-likely at 0xd9e87c8), goes into bits 29:25 of the word at +164 (mask 0xc1ffffff keeps the rest), and bit 9 (0x200) of the word at +160 is set.
- `__glOdysseyTextureSetDetailMode` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd9e8814, 168 bytes):
  **Established.** Takes the texture state in a0 and a mode in a1. The mode 260 (0x104, the add enumerant) stores a1 at +68 (+0x44) and clears bit 31 of the word at +164; the mode 8448 (0x2100, the modulate enumerant, tested with the branch-likely at 0xd9e8838) stores a1 at +68 and sets bit 31 of that word. Both accepted modes then set bit 9 (0x200) of the word at +160. Any other mode calls `__glSetError` with 1280 (local GOT entry 1490) and changes nothing.
- `__glOdysseyTextureSetMaxLOD` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd9e83b0, 172 bytes):
  **Established.** Works from the texture state in a0. It converts the difference of the words at +96 (+0x60) and +92 to a float and takes the smaller of that and the float at +88, limits the result to the range -15.0 to 15.0 (0x41700000 and 0xc1700000), multiplies by 256.0 (0x43800000), truncates to an integer and stores its low 14 bits at +204. It also sets bit 14 (0x4000) of the word at +160. The three limit compares are branch-likely. No calls.
- `__glOdysseyVisualsMatch` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd9ec0b8, 160 bytes):
  **Established.** Compares two visual records (a1 and a3) against the context (a0) and returns 1 or 0 in v0 (the low byte of v1). Both records' words at +84 (+0x54) must be zero and the first record's word at +0 must equal the context word at +3216, or the result is 0. Then, when the first record's byte at +4 is nonzero, the second record's byte at +4 must also be nonzero and the words at +40 must be equal. When the first record's byte at +4 is zero, the words at +36 are compared instead and the second record's byte is not read. a2 is only spilled.
- `__glOdysseyImageLogBase2` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd9d1eb0, 52 bytes):
  **Established.** Returns the base-2 logarithm of a0 rounded up: 0 for 0 and 1, 1 for 2, 2 for 3 and 4, 3 for 5 to 8. A nonzero value that is not a power of two (a0 and a0-1 not zero) starts the count at 1; the loop then shifts a0 right until it reaches zero, adding one per shift in the delay slot of the `bne`. No calls.
- `__glOdysseyInitFragmentMaterial` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd98aae0, 8 bytes):
  **Established.** Returns at once; its only other instruction, in the delay slot, stores a0 into the caller's outgoing argument word at 0(sp). Nothing in the context is written, so in this build the routine performs no initialisation.
- `__glOdysseyPickDepthProcs` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd9d7520, 8 bytes):
  **Established.** Returns at once with a0 stored to the caller's outgoing argument word at 0(sp) in the delay slot. It selects no depth routine and writes nothing to the context.
- `__glOdysseySoftClipPickClipProcs` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd9d7d60, 32 bytes):
  **Established.** Stores two routine addresses from local GOT entries 757 and 754 into the context in a0: `__glOdysseySoftClipClipLine` (0xda021b0) at +12540 (+0x30fc) and `__glOdysseySoftClipClipTriangle` (0xda01e68) at +12144, in that order. There is no condition; the fast variant (0xd9d7d80) differs only in storing `__glOdysseySoftClipClipTriangleFast` (0xda02150, entry 756) at +12144.
- `__glOdysseySoftClipPickLineProcs` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd9d7da0, 24 bytes):
  **Established.** Stores the address of `__glOdysseySoftClipRenderLine` (0xda00860, local GOT entry 744) into the context word at +12528 (+0x30f0). Unconditional, no calls. The point version (0xd9d7db8) stores `__glOdysseySoftClipRenderPoint` (0xda00ec0, entry 746) at +12552, and the triangle versions (0xd9d7dd0 and the fast one at 0xd9d7de8) store `__glOdysseySoftClipRenderTriangle` (0xda004c0, entry 742) and `__glOdysseySoftClipRenderTriangleFast` (0xd9fe7f4, entry 733) at +12128.
- `__glOdysseyPickRenderBitmapProcs` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd9d758c, 92 bytes):
  **Established.** Always stores `__glOdysseyRenderBitmap` (0xd96358c, local GOT entry 100) at context +12568 (+0x3118) (in the delay slot of the first test). It stores `__glFastDrawBitmap` (0xda42338, entry 1370) at +12564 only when the context words at +1192, +1196 and +1200 and the byte at +1189 are all zero, and `__glDrawBitmap` (0xda42220, entry 1369) at +12564 otherwise.
- `__glOdysseyPickPixelProcs` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd9d7528, 100 bytes):
  **Established.** Stores five routine addresses into the context in a0, in the order +12616 (+0x3148) `__glOdysseyDrawPixels` (0xd993cf8), +12640 `__glOdysseyConvolutionFilter` (0xd990cb4), +12644 `__glOdysseySeparableFilter` (0xd9913dc), +12648 `__glSlowPickGetConvolutionFilter` (0xda31de4) and +12652 `__glOdysseyGetSeparableFilter` (0xd991e64), then calls `__glOdysseySetCurrentFilter` (0xd9b5f84, local GOT entry 370) with the context and the value 2 and returns its result.
- `__glOdyim_EdgeFlag` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd9ec8f0, 52 bytes):
  **Established.** Tests the low byte of a0 and writes one FIFO word to the absolute address 0x10000: the count 15 when the byte is nonzero, 16 when it is zero, shifted left by 24 and ORed with 0x2000c02e, that is 0x2f00c02e or 0x3000c02e. Returns nothing. The vector form `__glOdyim_EdgeFlagv` (0xd9ec924) reads the byte at 0(a0) instead and sends the same two words under the same test.
- `CubicBezierFitConstant` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd982610, 40 bytes):
  **Established.** Takes a source array of floats in a0 and an output array of four floats in a1. It stores the source word at +4 into output +0 and zero into outputs +4, +8 and +12 (+0xc); the store order is +4, +0, +8, +12. `CubicBezierFitLinear` (0xd982638) differs: output +0 gets source +4, output +4 gets source +12 minus source +4 (`sub.s`), and +8 and +12 are zeroed; there the store order is +0, +8, +12, +4. Neither calls anything.
- `Det3` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd982744, 112 bytes):
  **Established.** Returns in f0 the determinant of a 3 by 3 matrix whose nine float arguments are, in o32 order, f12, f14, a2, a3 and the caller's stack words 16 to 32, read as rows (1 2 3), (4 5 6), (7 8 9): arg1*(arg5*arg9 - arg6*arg8) - arg2*(arg4*arg9 - arg6*arg7) + arg3*(arg4*arg8 - arg5*arg7), with the products taken in single precision (`mul.s`, `sub.s`, `add.s`). The frame is 8 bytes, which holds a2, a3 and arg5 for reuse.
- `floatTo12` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd9e1ad4, 108 bytes):
  **Established.** Converts the float in f12 to a 12-bit fixed-point integer: when the value, widened to double, is at or below 0.0 it returns 0 (the `bc1fl` at 0xd9e1af8 is the only branch-likely), otherwise it multiplies by 4096.0 (0x45800000), truncates, and returns the result limited to 4095 (a signed compare against 4096; the `mtc1` at 0xd9e1b08 is unreachable).
- `__glOdyim_PixelTransferf` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd99099c, 60 bytes):
  **Established.** Passes both arguments through to `__glim_PixelTransferf` (0xda1123c, local GOT entry 986) and returns its result. The float in a1 is moved through f12 and back to a1 (`mtc1`, `mfc1`) with its bits unchanged. `__glOdyim_PixelTransferi` (0xd9909d8) converts its integer a1 to float with `cvt.s.w` and calls `__glOdyim_PixelTransferf` (local GOT entry 305).
- `__glOdysseySPBalloc` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd977e10, 84 bytes):
  **Established.** Reads a count through the pointer in a1. A count below 1024 (unsigned) is raised to 1024 and written back through a1; the branch-likely at 0xd977e30 skips that for larger counts. It then calls the function at offset 0 of the structure in a0 with a0 unchanged and a1 set to the count times 4, and returns its result.
- `__glOdysseySPBfree` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd977e64, 56 bytes):
  **Established.** Calls the function at offset 12 of the structure in a0, leaving a0 and the other argument registers unchanged, and returns its result.
- `__glOdysseySPCompMachdep` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd977e9c, 104 bytes):
  **Established.** Calls `makeDLState` (0xd977bc4) with a0 and a1 unchanged, then reads the word at +20 (+0x14) of the record in a1: when it is nonzero the record's word at +8 is set to 0xd977a68 (`ExecuteDlist`), when zero to 0xd977b84 (`ExecuteDlistNomem`). Both addresses are formed as the word at gp-32740 (0xd970000) plus 31336 and 31620. It returns what `makeDLState` returned in v0.
- `__glOdysseyGetFilter` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd9b5f3c, 72 bytes):
  **Established.** Maps the enumerant in a0 to the address of one filter record inside the context held in process word 4112: 0x8010 gives context +768, 0x8011 gives +852 and 0x8012 gives +936. Any other value returns 0. The additions for +852 and +936 sit in the delay slots of the second and third compares; the routine has no frame and no calls.
- `__glOdysseySetCurrentFilter` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd9b5f84, 176 bytes):
  **Established.** Takes the context in a0 and a selector in a1, and starts from a dummy filter (the address in local GOT entry at gp-17044, `__glDummyFilter` 0xdb07350) with a zero companion value in a2. Selector 1 tests bit 25 of the context word at +1792 and, when it is set, picks the record at +768 with the companion context +63420 (0xf7bc). Selectors 2 and 3 take one path: bit 26 of the same word set picks the record at +852 with companion context +66332 (0x1031c); otherwise bit 27 (0x08000000) set picks +936 with companion context +69244 (0x10e7c); neither leaves the dummy. Any other selector leaves the dummy. The chosen filter address is stored at context +764 and the companion address at context +72684 (0x11bec), and the filter address is returned. Three of the tests are branch-likely (0xd9b5fac, 0xd9b5fc8, 0xd9b5fe8), and 0xd9b6004 is unreachable.
- `__glOdysseyInitLerpTree` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd9b5eb0, 140 bytes):
  **Established.** Clears 720 bytes at a0 with `memset` (global GOT slot 3931, gp-17028; a0 is saved in the frame and reloaded after the call), then stores 1.0 (0x3f800000) as sixteen float words at offsets 588, 584, 444, 480, 440, 436, 296, 332, 292, 288, 148, 184, 36, 0, 140 and 144, in that order. Nothing is returned.
- `__glOdysseyRasterPos2` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd9dca64, 96 bytes):
  **Established.** Takes the context in a0 and a pointer to two floats in a1. It builds a four-float vertex in the frame at sp+32: the two source floats, 0.0 for the third, and the float at context +3544 for the fourth. It then calls `__glOdysseyRasterPosGeneral` (0xd9dc480, the word at gp-32712 minus 15232) with the context, the address of that vertex and the count 2, and returns its result.
- `__glOdysseyImageCalcOdyEyeSpaceRasterPosZ` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd9d22e8, 168 bytes):
  **Established.** Takes no argument and reads the context from process word 4112. It forms two float dot products of the four floats at context +256, +260, +264 and +268 with the rows at context +38312 to +38324 and +38344 to +38356, each as products added in the order first, second, third, fourth with `mul.s` and `add.s`. It divides the first sum by the second in single precision, adds the double constant 0x3ea0c6f7a0b5ed8d (about 5.0e-7, at 0xdaeed50) after widening, narrows the sum to float and returns it in f0. The second sum is also stored to 4(sp) and never read.
- `__glOdysseyImageTilingFree` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd9d167c, 132 bytes):
  **Established.** Does nothing when a0 is zero. Otherwise it takes the context from process word 4112 and calls the function at context +12 with the context and the record's word at +8, zeroes the record's words at +0, +4 and +8, and calls the same function at context +12 again with the context and the record itself. The routine returns that second call's result.
- `__glOdyim_EdgeFlagv` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd9ec924, 52 bytes):
  **Established.** Reads the byte at the pointer in a0 and sends one FIFO word to the absolute address 0x10000: count 15 for a nonzero byte, 16 for zero, shifted left 24 and ORed with 0x2000c02e, so the word is 0x2f00c02e or 0x3000c02e. Unlike the scalar form it reads memory rather than a register; it has no frame and returns nothing.
- `CubicBezierFitLinear` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd982638, 48 bytes):
  **Established.** Takes a source float array in a0 and writes four floats to a1: source +4 to output +0, the difference of source +12 and source +4 to output +4 (`sub.s`), and zero to +8 and +12. The stores are issued in the order +0, +8, +12, +4. The routine makes no calls.
- `__glOdyim_PixelTransferi` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd9909d8, 72 bytes):
  **Established.** Converts the integer value in a1 to a single-precision float with `cvt.s.w` and calls `__glOdyim_PixelTransferf` (0xd99099c, local GOT entry 305) with a0 unchanged and the float's bits in a1. a2 receives the original integer on the way but the callee does not use it. - `__glOdysseySoftClipPickClipProcsFast` (`builds/6.5.30-media/usr/gfx/arch/IP30ODSY/libGLcore.so` 0xd9d7d80, 32 bytes):
